- **testdoc-gaps**: Turns a coverage file (lcov.info, Cobertura XML or coverage.py JSON) into a ranked "write these tests first" list: files ordered by how much of them is untested, the uncovered line ranges, branches never taken and functions never called, optionally limited to files changed in a git diff. - **testdoc-lint**: Scans test files (Python unittest and pytest, JavaScript and TypeScript Jest, Vitest and Mocha, Go, Java JUnit) for tests that cannot fail or that hide failures: no assertions, assertTrue(True), not-None-only checks, empty bodies, expect() with no matcher, skips without a reason, sleeps, swallowed exceptions, commented-out assertions and snapshot-only tests.
